OpenXR: Initialize projection layer transfer size from creation data

OpenXrCompositionLayer ignored read_only_data().texture_width and
texture_height for Type::kProjection layers, leaving transfer_size_ zero
until SetSwapchainImageSize() defaulted it to the full OpenXR swapchain
size. As a result, SetEnabledCompositionLayers() initially allocated all
SharedImages and AHardwareBuffers at the full unscaled swapchain size
before reallocating them on subsequent frames after UpdateLayerBounds()
updated the transfer size.

Initialize transfer_size_ from read_only_data().texture_width and
texture_height for Type::kProjection layers, and delete any existing
shared_buffer_texture GL texture ID before generating a new one in
OpenXrGraphicsBindingOpenGLES::ResizeSharedBuffer().

R=bajones@chromium.org, bsheedy@chromium.org, ruofei@google.com

[Autofill] Fix eWallet account linking bottom sheet height UI blip

When EwalletAccountLinkingManager completes with kResultOk, it leaves
the progress bottom sheet open so PaymentLinkManager can reuse it during
the subsequent checkout phase, and resets its own ui_state_ to kHidden.
However, PaymentLinkManager::OnAccountLinkingResult did not update
PaymentLinkManager::ui_state_ to kProgressScreen, causing
DismissProgressScreen() to be a no-op when PurchaseManager opened. This
left Chrome's bottom sheet stuck behind PurchaseManager, causing a
slight UI blip where two overlapping bottom sheet heights were visible.

[Current UI
screenshot](https://screenshot-v2.corp.google.com/6dbffu8847hog)

[UI screenshot with this CL's
fix](https://screenshot-v2.corp.google.com/5prvdllun5alg)

[Video
demo](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1vqLVaTdIhp8ej3hKAx8y8bEhOM_3nXJy/view?usp=sharing)

This CL:
1. Updates PaymentLinkManager::OnAccountLinkingResult to reset
   ewallet_account_linking_manager_ once at entry and set
   ui_state_ = kProgressScreen on kResultOk.
2. Updates PaymentLinkManager::Reset to call DismissPrompt() when
   ui_state_ != kHidden so early exits automatically close any open
   bottom sheet.
3. Restores the 16dp top margin above the Google Pay logo in
   ewallet_account_linking_prompt.xml after the standard handlebar
   migration (https://crrev.com/c/8358596).

Extensions: Re-enable WebRequestCORSWithExtraHeaders on Mac and CrOS

WebRequestCORSWithExtraHeaders was disabled on Mac and ChromeOS due to
flakiness caused by testCorsPreflightWithExtraHeaders in test_cors.js
not waiting for the preflight's onCompleted and the actual request's
onErrorOccurred events before advancing to the next subtest.

https://crrev.com/c/8262045 restored those missing expectations so the
subtest waits for the full request chain to complete. Re-enable the test
on Mac and ChromeOS.

[VCN] Move onAccept and onCancel delegate calls to mediator

This CL moves onAccept and onCancel delegate calls from the
AutofillVcnEnrollBottomSheetCoordinator to the Mediator.

This improves MVC separation by keeping interaction logic encapsulated
within the Mediator rather than split across Coordinator and Mediator.
This also keeps things consistent with other bottom sheet mediators.

[iOS] Remove AppLaunchManager observer at start of -tearDown

This CL solves `testSignInDisabledAtStartupWithManagedAccount`'s
flakiness. This flakiness was caused by an observer that was not removed
before the app restarted. This CL also contains a regression test that
checks this crash cause specifically.

This analysis and the fix are generated by antigravity.

`-[ChromeTestCase setUp]` registers `self` as an
`AppLaunchManagerObserver` before calling `[super setUp]`. However,
`-[ChromeTestCase tearDown]` previously called `[self tearDownHelper]`
and `[ChromeEarlGrey closeAllExtraWindows]` before removing `self` from
`[AppLaunchManager sharedManager]`.

Whenever an earlier test in a shard failed to launch or crashed the host
app without allowing crashes, `[ChromeEarlGrey closeAllExtraWindows]`
threw a `GREYFrameworkException` during `-tearDown`, aborting
`-tearDown` before `[[AppLaunchManager sharedManager]
removeObserver:self]` could run. Because XCTest retains `XCTestCase`
instances across the suite, the failed test's `ChromeTestCase` remained
registered as a dangling observer for all subsequent tests in the shard.

When any subsequent test relaunched the app with startup paused at the
background stage (`--test-pause-startup=Background`, such as
`SigninCoordinatorTestCase/testSignInDisabledAtStartupWithManagedAccount`,
`SigninCoordinatorTestCase/testSignInDisabledAtStartupWithPersonalAccount`,
or `StartupMetricsTestCase`), the leaked observer received
`-appLaunchManagerDidRelaunchApp:runResets:` and invoked
`+[ChromeTestCase enableMockAuthentication]` -> `[ChromeEarlGrey
setUpFakeSyncServer]` -> `chrome_test_util::GetOriginalProfile()`,
crashing the host app on
`CHECK(GetForegroundActiveScene().profileState.profile)` before scenes
reached the foreground.

Move `[[AppLaunchManager sharedManager] removeObserver:self]` to the
very beginning of `-[ChromeTestCase tearDown]`, add a regression test in
`chrome_test_case_egtest.mm`, and re-enable
`testSignInDisabledAtStartupWithManagedAccount`.

Fixed: 570563895

ash: Supersede in-flight IME file download when requested URL changes

Switching input methods tears down the previous decoder instance and its
pending download in the IME service while leaving `url_loader_` running
in the browser process (e.g. a background download of `en-840` started
at login). Previously, `ImeServiceConnector::MaybeTriggerDownload`
rejected any new download for a different URL while `url_loader_` was
active, causing the newly activated input method's language pack
download to fail permanently for the session.

Abort the superseded download and notify its listeners before starting
the new download request.

TAG=agy
CONV=f6092364-c4a1-4c86-9e60-c3591d5abfb3

Deflake NetworkConnectionTrackerBrowserTest.SimulateNetworkServiceCrash

parent
86f8d2c397c9d084d97e1b39b87b5107bf0a2057 [ diff ] 
Deflake NetworkConnectionTrackerBrowserTest.SimulateNetworkServiceCrash

SimulateNetworkServiceCrash() returns once its own NetworkServiceTest
pipe disconnects, but the NetworkConnectionTracker may not have observed
that its own pipe to the crashed network service closed yet. In that
case GetConnectionType() synchronously returns the CONNECTION_3G cached
from the crashed network service instead of waiting for the tracker to
re-register with the restarted one, so the 2G change can be broadcast
before the tracker is listening. OnInitialConnectionType() doesn't
notify observers, so WaitForNotification() then hangs until the test
times out.

Wait until the tracker stops reporting the stale CONNECTION_3G before
issuing the GetConnectionType() request. The restarted network service
seeds its connection type from the real NetworkChangeNotifier, so this
happens as soon as the tracker drops its stale state: either its type is
invalid while it reconnects (and the existing GetConnectionType() wait
covers the re-registration), or it already received the restarted
service's initial connection type.

ash: Stop 288 more files from compiling the cryptohome proto header

Follow-up that stops pulling UserDataAuth.pb.h into another 288 source
files, so 216 are left, down from 504 (1,267 before the previous
change). On 50 randomly picked affected files, compile time fell by
0.94 s per file (6.6%), all 50 got faster, and peak memory fell 3.6%.
Extrapolated to all 288 files, that is about 4.5 CPU-minutes per full
build, or about 17 together with the previous change, on the same
workstation. The saving varies with machine and build setup.

Why: cryptohome::ErrorWrapper in error_types.h held the proto by value,
so every file using the error type got the whole generated header.
Three auth headers also included it for declarations only.

Fix: hold the proto in a std::unique_ptr with out-of-line methods and
a deep copy, keeping the same API. Forward-declare the types in the
three auth headers and include the proto where it is really used.

Test: autoninja chrome unit_tests browser_tests interactive_ui_tests
ash_unittests chromeos_unittests ash_components_unittests
components_unittests; gn check

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in ProtectedMediaIdentifierPermissionContext

In certain runtime scenarios, such as WebUI queries for site settings,
GetContentSettingStatusInternal can return unexpected content settings
that trigger a CHECK failure in
ProtectedMediaIdentifierPermissionContext. This changes the CHECK back
to a DCHECK to prevent browser crashes.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/4795f7c408400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

ash: Stop 763 files from compiling the 3 MB cryptohome proto header

Stop pulling the large generated UserDataAuth.pb.h header (3 MB) into
source files that don't use it. In a ChromeOS Chrome build, the number
of source files that include it drops from 1,267 to 504. On 50 randomly
picked affected files, compile time fell by 0.95 s per file (7%), all
50 got faster, and peak memory fell 3.5%. Extrapolated to all 763
files, that is about 12 CPU-minutes per full build on the workstation
used for the measurement. The saving varies with machine and build
setup.

Why: eight widely included cryptohome and auth headers included
UserDataAuth.pb.h only to name a few proto types in declarations.

Fix: forward-declare those types in the headers, and include
UserDataAuth.pb.h in the .cc files that really use them, with the
matching BUILD.gn deps.

Test: autoninja chrome unit_tests browser_tests interactive_ui_tests
ash_unittests chromeos_unittests ash_components_unittests
components_unittests; gn check

tree
e1765769f033a9017b36e11ad802b8a8af8c8193
parent
19f7aba914aa00146d4b131f99989b5c3227578a [ diff ] 
[remoting] Re-enable NativeMessagingReaderTest.ReaderDestroyedByOwner

NativeMessagingReader::Core::ReadMessage() posted InvokeMessageCallback
to the caller thread before looping back to call ReadAtCurrentPos() for
the next message. On Windows, if ~NativeMessagingReader() ran before the
reader thread entered ReadFile(), CancelSynchronousIo() returned
ERROR_NOT_FOUND and the reader thread subsequently blocked forever in
ReadFile() during thread join.

Track stop_reading_ and in_read_ with sequentially consistent atomics in
Core::ReadData(), and retry CancelSynchronousIo() while in_read_ is true
during ~NativeMessagingReader() on Windows.

Verified with 100 local iterations (--gtest_repeat=100) and 6 consecutive
runs on win-rel alongside Linux, ChromeOS, Mac, ASan, and TSan trybots.

[Linux] Remember declined GlobalShortcuts portal shortcuts
📋 Metadata
commit
97682c0193332439e40e6e293a3ee0dd910ed743
[ log ]
author
Thomas Anderson <thomasanderson@chromium.org>

committer

Thu Oct 22 04:12:21 2026
tree
b7f78313e95f5f405e90734a81e81e9b73313317
parent
db28a53ea06788cbc12b89a0e1677bfbaedc2e68 [ diff ] 
[Linux] Remember declined GlobalShortcuts portal shortcuts

Cancelling the GlobalShortcuts portal dialog wasn't remembered, so it
was shown on every browser start until accepted. On desktops where
binding can never succeed (e.g. niri, which uses
xdg-desktop-portal-gnome without GNOME Shell and responds with code 2),
it was shown on every start indefinitely.

When a bind fails with response code 1 (cancelled by the user) or 2
(the interaction ended in some other way), mark the new shortcuts as
declined, and bind again without them so that previously accepted
shortcuts stay active. If the user cancels a bind of only previously
accepted shortcuts (e.g. because the portal's stored shortcuts were
reset), mark those as declined instead. Previously accepted shortcuts
aren't declined on code 2, since a backend may respond with it without
showing a dialog.

Declined shortcuts are offered again when their accelerator changes or
the command is made global again, so a shortcut declined because of a
transient code 2 failure can be recovered.

Bug: 404298968

tree
e4d2572c6acb0809d6a4e1ee3838b810f6956fca
parent
ee34496055826983723854b86ddd29a95adb209a [ diff ] 
[WebNN] Support multiple EPs in --webnn-ort-ep-library-path-for-testing

Allow the switch to take multiple <ep_name>?<ep_library_path> pairs
separated by ",", so that several development EP builds (e.g. OpenVINO
and WebGPU) can be injected at the same time. A single pair keeps
working as before. Each EP name may only appear once, and any invalid
pair (blank field, unknown or duplicated EP name) disables the whole
switch.

The parsing and injection logic is moved into
AddTestingEpsFromCommandLine() so that it can be unit tested.

committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Oct 22 04:05:13 2026
tree
a65324c52ab49fb821fdc8177d4ba02894d69d28
parent
196b8dcfc2eafaca8dbb955096993495d91e5998 [ diff ] 
[Vertical Tabs] Close tab on middle-click or 3-finger touchpad tap

Horizontal tab strip items handle tertiary button clicks (middle mouse
click or 3-finger touchpad tap) by closing the tab, whereas vertical tab
rows previously routed all clicks to the primary tab selection listener.

Capture BUTTON_TERTIARY on ACTION_DOWN in
TabVerticalViewBinder.setNullableClickListener (since Android clears the
button state on ACTION_UP before OnClickListener fires) and invoke the
tab's close action listener instead of selecting the tab.

tree
668ad7d0c53202bc370b8f91b6bc44c6d0b5f2ec
parent
0eb73c4208994a9ecfb48fe08c35fef9671196fc [ diff ] 
[Task Manager] Remove tasks unconditionally in OnTaskToBeRemoved

OnTaskToBeRemoved() previously returned early if ShouldKeepTask(id) was
false. However, ShouldKeepTask(id) can return false for a task that is
present in tasks_ (e.g. when a parent task is removed before its child
subframe task in WebContentsEntry::ClearAllTasks, or when a search
filter is active and matched_process_set_ is updated). Returning early
left a destroyed TaskId in tasks_, causing a CHECK failure when
TableView later accessed the stale row.

Remove the ShouldKeepTask(id) check and update matched_process_set_
after erasing id from tasks_.

Bug: 396002122
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*ClearTagWithSubframesRemovesAllTasks*:*RemoveTaskWhileFilteredBySearchTerm*

tree
8a38323eee997b84953da138805138c2c4f94c9f
parent
2b6072eed526b3ed4bc11bb668b6448881dc3dfd [ diff ] 
[WebUI] Replace unpack_component_extension with build_webui :build_zip target

continuing tikuta@'s crrev.com/c/8376442

When building with -strict_remote -config=default-remote, the
unpack_component_extension action failed because it reached into
build_webui()'s internal .grd and intermediate output files without
declaring them as inputs.

Instead of exposing the .grd file and intermediate outputs outside
build_webui(), this change:

- Adds a new generate_zip() rule (generate_zip.gni / generate_zip.py)
  and unit tests under //ui/webui/resources/tools/ to package files
  referenced by a .grd file into a deterministic .zip archive.

- Adds an optional generate_zip parameter (default false) to
  build_webui() to expose a public :build_zip target producing
  $target_gen_dir/resources.zip.

- Replaces unpack_component_extension("unpack_extension") in
  //chrome/browser/resources/omnibox_internals/aim_eligibility_extension
  with generate_zip = true on build_webui("build"), removes the custom
  unpack_component_extension.{gni,py} scripts and Siso denylist entry,
  and updates README.md to use the :build_zip target.

Test: out/Default/bin/run_webui_resources_tools_python_unittests
Bug: b:525368663, b:513105742

b0217cd7c47edc7d9a643bbc592314650fd274d4

0d59f4f86bc74d5ad20c4422950655a8a813255b
parent
bea2ef4f39f01ba1e8352a5d40d9571a43dc26c2 [ diff ] 
[media] Fix >8 channel layout and residue bounds in Symphonia Vorbis

1. Per Section 4.3.9 of the Vorbis I specification, channel counts
   greater than 8 have application-defined (discrete) channel order
   rather than predefined speaker positions. Update
   vorbis_channels_to_channels in symphonia-common to return
   Channels::Discrete(u16::from(num_channels)) for non-zero channel
   counts above 8 instead of None.

2. In symphonia-codec-vorbis residue decoding, decode_classes relies on
   out.len() to determine how many trailing class assignments in the
   final classword exceed the remaining partitions (parts_per_classword
   > out.len()). Passing unbounded upper slices (&mut
   self.part_classes[part_first + i * parts_to_read..] in
   read_residue_inner_type_0_1 and &mut self.part_classes[part_first..]
   in read_residue_inner_type_2) caused decode_classes on the final
   batch of pass 0 to overflow into channel i + 1's partition classes
   (or the old long-block tail), corrupting codebook lookups and
   bitstream synchronization in pass >= 1. Bound the slices to
   ..parts_to_read and ..(i + 1) * parts_to_read.

3. Enable AudioFileReaderTest.VorbisValidChannelLayout (9ch.ogg) for
   Symphonia now that 9+ channel Vorbis decoding is supported.

Avoid fixed delay between PDF response-body reads
📋 Metadata
commit
f977ae91247cfcd1a5b7b5e91e31a80c94661a93
[ log ]
author

Thu Oct 22 03:40:56 2026
committer

Thu Oct 22 03:40:56 2026
tree
6b6010ecf49842f0f82b66fc2bc27a7a5d79c025
parent
e4976583fa741cefd408d66266f79677429c44b4 [ diff ] 
Avoid fixed delay between PDF response-body reads

The PDF URL loader wrapper currently waits 2 ms before starting every
response-body read. This delay was introduced in 2016 to yield to the
main thread and reduce CPU usage.

At that time, Chromium classified short delayed tasks as high-resolution
on Windows, so a 2 ms delay generally ran close to its requested
deadline. The task scheduler has since changed to favor power
efficiency: ordinary flexible delayed tasks receive scheduling leeway,
and high-resolution Windows timers are now enabled only for precise
tasks. Consequently, the 2 ms OneShotTimer can commonly take
approximately one 15.6 ms Windows timer interval to fire.

This becomes a significant bottleneck when response data is already
buffered, because the delay is paid once per read. Traces show repeated
renderer-main-thread idle gaps attributed to this timer rather than PDF
parsing or painting.

Schedule the read by posting a CancelableOnceClosure with no delay. This
preserves the asynchronous boundary that prevents immediately completed
buffered reads from recursively continuing on the current call stack,
while removing the fixed wall-clock wait.

Add coverage verifying that initiating a read does not complete it
synchronously.

tree
98ff459a16fe886e7752e75dcd247c791e871d62
parent
408319713b0f48e37024afcb91917cdb3054313c [ diff ] 
OpenXR: Increase swapchain image wait timeout to 1000ms

Heavy WebGPU WebXR workloads at native resolution can exceed 50ms of
GPU time per frame once all swapchain images are in flight. When
xrWaitSwapchainImage times out with XR_TIMEOUT_EXPIRED, retrying
xrWaitSwapchainImage on the Android OpenXR runtime fails with
XR_ERROR_CALL_ORDER_INVALID, aborting BeginFrame() and breaking frame
submission.

Increase kSwapchainWaitTimeout from 50ms to 1000ms so heavy GPU frames
do not trigger false-positive swapchain wait timeouts while still
remaining well below Android's 5-second ANR threshold on session
shutdown.

R=bajones@chromium.org, bsheedy@chromium.org, ruofei@google.com

baad542fcbf29c13d73e45c99523e6967a49cb6e
[ log ]
author
Keigo Oka <oka@google.com>

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Oct 22 03:18:59 2026
tree
57ec59c792390fc250809fd2bccf0e02f4652223
parent
7a205e22e2edad5132e0d77b0b034ea601029301 [ diff ] 
Media: Migrate MediaCodecBridge to JniRawPtr

MediaCodecBridge stores its native ::media::MediaCodecBridgeImpl pointer
in a raw Java long field. Passing untyped integer handles across the JNI
boundary bypasses compile-time type checking and lifetime tracking,
risking use-after-free bugs when the native object is destroyed.

Declare a package-private NativeMediaCodecBridge JniTypeToken mapped
to ::media::MediaCodecBridgeImpl and store the borrowed reference in a
JniRawPtr<NativeMediaCodecBridge> created via jni_zero::MakeRaw.
Pass the reference to @NativeMethods calls via
JniPtr<NativeMediaCodecBridge> and release the handle on native
teardown.

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

ime: Preserve pending commit and apply pending composition range in ConfirmComposition

Previously, InputMethodAsh::ConfirmComposition ignored
pending_composition_range_, skipped applying pending_composition_ if
pending_commit_ was also present, and cleared pending_commit_ via
ResetContext(). Additionally, ResetContext() did not reset
pending_composition_range_.

ConfirmCompositionText(), preserve pending_commit_ across ResetContext()
in ConfirmComposition(), and clear pending_composition_range_ in
ResetContext().

parent
35c816556d6c2b0ef240646e7e29ee7a91382d57 [ diff ] 
Remove disassociated tab context in contextual tasks extension

In the side panel rearchitecture, tab strip underlines already clear on
navigation via ActiveTaskContextProviderImpl, and disassociating the
only tab of a task already destroys the cached side panel WebContents
via CleanUpUnusedWebContents(). However, when multiple tabs share a task
and one tab is disassociated (closed, or navigated away while the side
panel is closed), the task's side panel WebContents and session handle
stay alive for the remaining associated tabs.

Observe OnTaskDisassociatedFromTab() in
ContextualTasksWebContentsUserData and call DeleteContext() so that in
multi-tab tasks:
1. Unsubmitted tab selections are removed from GetSelectedTabs(),
   unmounting the input-plate context library chip when no selected tabs
   remain, unchecking the tab in the tab picker, and dropping the tab
   from suggest inputs and the next query submission.
2. Previously submitted tabs are moved into displaced_submitted_tabs_ so
   their request_id is sent in removed_contexts to AIM on the next
   follow-up query.

Bug: b:574118411
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=*ContextualTasksWebContentsUserDataTest*
Demo: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjczMTQ0OTcxMjUwODkyOHw1YzFmNmE4NS03MQ

8f7380fb67a4b12c7ca8150a549c2b30de8b70ed [ diff ] 
[UI] Rename `WindowOpenDisposition` enumerators to kCamelCase

Rename the enumerators of the mojom `ui.mojom.WindowOpenDisposition`
from SCREAMING_CASE to kCamelCase (e.g. CURRENT_TAB -> kCurrentTab), as
the Mojo style guide asks for, and update all C++ (including Blink) and
WebUI TypeScript users. Java is unaffected: its bindings keep generating
UPPER_SNAKE_CASE constants. The numeric values do not change.

This has to be done in a single CL because the C++ enum, its users and
the generated bindings must all switch names at once. Callers that still
use the `WindowOpenDisposition` alias keep compiling.

This CL was generated by running the following from src/, then
`git cl format`:

  migrate_window_open_disposition.py --rename-values .

The script is attached to https://crbug.com/373551504.

tree
1e4a7e421a71e3efc31577832be57681ce0fa5ee
parent
b5a1d0c8a969deb2857d0bfd026148ca0b1449c9 [ diff ] 
[crubit] Generate depfiles for `rust_api_from_cpp` actions.

Before this CL, incremental builds didn't rerun the
`rs_bindings_from_cc` action when a header that is only included
indirectly (i.e. not listed in `public_headers`) changed.

After this CL, `rust_api_from_cpp.gni` passes `-MD`, `-MF`, and `-MT`
to Clang and declares the `depfile` of the action.  The currently
rolled Crubit revision already supports these flags (support was added
in Crubit f5b9a778cba579300d2ef14b60fc02c2c074d8c6).

On Windows the flags need the `/clang:` prefix, because in
`--driver-mode=cl` `-MD` means `/MD`.  `libclang_args.gni` now exports
`clang_flag_prefix` (`"/clang:"` on Windows, `""` elsewhere) and uses
it for `-fparse-all-comments`, so that the two call sites share one
definition.

The action stays local-only in Siso.  In clean builds the depfile
doesn't exist yet, so remote execution doesn't receive the indirectly
included headers (`fatal error: '...' file not found`).  This is the
same limitation that `rust/bindgen` has (see b/356496947).  Siso
scandeps doesn't help, because the headers are passed via
`--public_headers=...` rather than as regular compiler inputs.

Manual test (Linux): build
`gen/build/rust/tests/test_rust_api_from_cpp/target_depending_on_another_rs_api.rs`,
edit `self_contained_target_header2.h` (included only indirectly),
and build again.  Before this CL the action didn't rerun.  After this
CL it reruns.  Windows was only checked with `gn desc` (the args
contain `/clang:-MD`, `/clang:-MF...`, `/clang:-MT...`).

tree
05721b199614fe2e20042f3d80a9eaa6a5819f0e
parent
903dd16ac33c448c6af69976ab41309999381f7a [ diff ] 
[rust] Use `cargo_crate` for `//third_party/crabbyavif` sys targets.

Before this CL, `//third_party/crabbyavif` used `rust_static_library`
with an explicit `crate_name` for `:crabbyavif_dav1d_sys` and
`:crabbyavif_libyuv_sys`.  `rust_static_library` is intended for
first-party code that uses auto-generated crate names and
`chromium::import!`.  Third-party code cannot use `chromium::import!`.

After this CL, `:crabbyavif_dav1d_sys` and `:crabbyavif_libyuv_sys`
use `cargo_crate`, which supports `crate_name` and applies third-party
defaults (no Chromium prelude, no clippy, warnings suppressed).
`build_native_rust_unit_tests = false` keeps today's behavior of not
building unit tests for these crates.

R=jzern@google.com, vigneshv@google.com

60a2ff4da4a9c51acd3695b6e6ac404fdac7e18f [ diff ] 
[WebAudio] Make AudioWorkletProcessorErrorDetails always hold an error

AudioWorkletProcessorErrorDetails used a kNoError sentinel to mean "no
failure", so every consumer had to exclude it, and each failure site
built the message and empty location by hand. The V8 exception helper
lived on AudioWorkletProcessor although AudioWorkletGlobalScope calls
it before any processor exists.

Drop kNoError: AudioWorkletProcessor now holds a std::optional of the
details, and the enum lists only the three failure kinds. Build every
details object through a single Create() that owns the message format
per kind, and extract V8 exception info where the TryCatch lives with
ExtractScriptException(). Use plain copies when posting across
threads: String is thread-safe and isolated copies are no longer
required (string_impl.h).

The process() fallback message now follows the "<name> process(): "
format; all other messages and locations are unchanged.

TAG=agy
CONV=1abd37a9-afdc-46c1-b03d-c2f6de3c4466

[WebAudio] Propagate AudioWorkletProcessor construction error details

Previously, AudioWorkletGlobalScope::CreateProcessor() discarded its
v8::TryCatch on failure and AudioWorkletHandler reported a generic
construction error with empty source location. CreateProcessor() also
invoked the user constructor with null options when options
deserialization failed.

error details to processorerror events across all failure paths:

  constructor returns a non-AudioWorkletProcessor.

7db4bd1b8bbd8789ea5af02ca78420f9922f7b7c

Thu Oct 22 02:48:41 2026
tree
7042283cfab6b3c01c070922357fe115c31950d4
parent
284999cbfbcc000ff8d25acacb29e26b63bbdf7a [ diff ] 
siso: set restat_content=False on perfetto/touch_file

Perfetto's perfetto_sql_source_set and perfetto_py_library GN templates
invoke third_party/perfetto/tools/touch_file.py with restat = 1 to
produce an empty 0-byte file representing their transitive .sql or .py
sources. Downstream code generation steps (gen_amalgamated_sql.py and
gen_tp_table_headers.py) depend on these 0-byte touch_file.py outputs
via GN deps without listing the transitive .sql or .py files in their
own Ninja inputs or writing a depfile.

Because https://crrev.com/c/8614211 makes restat_content treat
unchanged empty files as clean, enabling restat_content automatically
for restat = 1 steps would cause Siso to skip gen_amalgamated_sql.py
and gen_tp_table_headers.py when transitive .sql or .py sources change.

Set restat_content = False on perfetto/touch_file in linux.star and add
local-only perfetto/touch_file rules with restat_content = False in
mac.star and windows.star so that touch_file.py is opted out across all
host platforms without changing remote execution behavior on Mac or
Windows.

[MFVEA] Probe AMD AV1 encoders with level 6.0

The AMD AV1 HMFT fails AV1 level probe above 6.0 prior to any successful
SetOutputType() invocation.

Workaround to use level 6.0 for the probe: the supported framerates
and resolutions are derived from MF_VIDEO_MAX_MB_PER_SEC, and
InitializeInputOutputParameters() doesn't set a level at all.

[xr][gpu] Use WaitSyncTokenAndSignal in OpenXrRenderLoop on Linux

tree
4d354eccca9759e28e2ae93788e12c097d2fb43e
parent
14c2dffc98a647b9c5de4855af035828bf063879 [ diff ] 
[xr][gpu] Use WaitSyncTokenAndSignal in OpenXrRenderLoop on Linux

This CL is a follow-up of https://crrev.com/c/8471692 and a prerequisite
before enabling the UseAutomaticSyncTokenManagement feature.

Under automatic SyncToken management, ClientSharedImage::EndExport()
stores imported SyncTokens internally in the ClientSharedImage and
returns an empty SyncToken. As a result, waiting directly on
combined_sync_tokens in OpenXrRenderLoop::SubmitFrameDrawnIntoTexture()
on Linux is a no-op when automatic SyncToken management is enabled.

This CL adds ClientSharedImage::WaitSyncTokenAndSignal(), which waits on
the SyncTokens via WaitSyncTokensInternal(), flushes the GL context,
generates a SyncToken, and signals the callback via ContextSupport.
OpenXrRenderLoop::SubmitFrameDrawnIntoTexture() is updated to use this
helper on Linux so that it works in both manual and automatic SyncToken
modes.

b9790d06e87f080f995725448311ea51a58da1fe [ diff ] 
[ContextualTasks] Sync toolbar on theme changes

When the user changes the browser appearance or system color scheme
while the Contextual Tasks side panel is open, the persistent top-level
toolbar WebUI and side panel container previously kept their initial
colors until the panel was recreated. Refreshing side panel color
schemes in `ContextualTasksWebView::OnThemeChanged()` updates the
toolbar document's `prefers-color-scheme` media query directly,
avoiding custom Mojo plumbing.

Because `toolbar.html` never receives a `cs` query parameter in
production, toolbar dark mode styling is moved entirely to CSS
`prefers-color-scheme` media queries alongside `ColorChangeUpdater`
backed by `ThemeSource`, removing legacy URL parameter tracking from
`toolbar_app.ts`.

WebMCP: Fix ModelContextUserData lifetime for replacement navigations

The result of a javascript: URL, of an XSLT transform, or of discarding a
frame replaces the renderer's Document without a navigation that commits
in the browser. This means that DocumentUserData tied to the old
Document persist even after the Document in the renderer has changed.
This lifetime mismatch is likely an issue for all uses of
DocumentUserData, including ModelContextUserData used by WebMCP: when
the new Document used document.modelContext, its ModelContextHost bind
found the old Document's ModelContextUserData and the browser killed the
renderer with RFHI_WEBMCP_DUPLICATE_BIND.

This change adds an IPC for the renderer to notify the browser when a
replacement navigation commits in the renderer. The notification is used
to reset ModelContextUserData. A separate CL should look into resetting
all DocumentUserData for these navigations (crbug.com/576419973).

The new Document binds ModelContextHost through the frame's
BrowserInterfaceBroker, which isn't ordered with this IPC, so the bind
can still race it (crbug.com/576011705). The same race exists for the
first commit after the initial empty Document when its window is reused.

Bug: 555173199

📋 Metadata

tree
211f8289f46f0b79fb5137d293d5315245920ee5
parent
ccb8fd875717d1c8239677b7f716d93bf8d87696 [ diff ] 
Notify the drag handler window when a drag session ends

WaylandDataDragController uses the window with pointer or touch focus as
the origin window of an outgoing drag session, and notified that window
when the session ended. However, the drag loop runs in the window that
requested the drag via WaylandWindow::StartDrag(), which can be a
different window. For example, when the drag starts while the pointer
focus is on a bubble (a subsurface of the requesting window, such as the
omnibox suggestions popup), the bubble is the origin window. In that
case, WaylandWindow::OnDragSessionClose() ran on the bubble, which has
no drag finished callback, so the drag loop of the requesting window
never quit, and StartDragAndDrop() never returned. Since the drag client
still considered a drag to be in progress, it rejected all later drags.

This affects dragging text out of the omnibox while its suggestions
popup is open, which intermittently hits this case:
- With the native omnibox, the text can still be selected afterwards,
but no longer dragged.
- With the WebUI toolbar, the source renderer never gets
DragSourceSystemDragEnded(), so it ignores all input from then on, and
the whole toolbar freezes.

Pass the requesting window to StartSession() and notify it, rather than
the origin window, when the session ends. The origin window is still
used as the origin surface of wl_data_device.start_drag, as the protocol
requires the surface that has the implicit grab.

Tests: add WaylandDataDragControllerTest.
StartDragWithPointerFocusOnBubble_{Cancel,Drop,BubbleDestroyed}, which
hang without this fix.

Bug: 572001190

tree
24ef9a8aa3e83841a59d33139626b3e9ce7f29d1
parent
9ea7576151d6fbcc99f89368549e6f2b30093d02 [ diff ] 
Prevent premature garbage collection in WebGPU context lost tests

Anchor the GPUDevice to the window in WebGPU context lost test pages
to prevent it from being garbage collected while awaiting device.lost.

Previously, `device` was scoped locally within the test functions and
was not referenced after awaiting `device.lost`. Under aggressive
garbage collection timing (such as with V8LimitBasedMemoryReducer
enabled), the device was garbage collected before device loss
dispatched, causing the promise to never resolve and resulting in 60s
harness timeouts.

[MediaCapabilities] Enforce mime type restrictions in IsValidMimeType()

Per the W3C Media Capabilities specification, a valid media MIME type
must either imply a single codec without parameters, or specify a single
codec in the codecs parameter, and application/* MIME types must match
the audio or video configuration type.

Spec: https://w3c.github.io/media-capabilities/#check-mime-type-validity

Update IsValidMimeType() accordingly, add unit tests, and delete
obsolete WPT expectation files.

34841b4c43ae0045446f8d0dac02c6cf1fa20bcb
parent
2f1ca0a955ad94b264765369b4dcffa071e5b5af [ diff ] 
Make profiles without a policy connector ineligible for cloud upload

On ChromeOS, the Office upload and OneDrive checks run for every frame
when its interface binders are registered. After
IsEligibleAndEnabledUploadOfficeToCloud(),
IsMicrosoftOfficeOneDriveIntegrationAllowed() uses the policy connector
of the profile without a null check.

Return false in IsEligibleAndEnabledUploadOfficeToCloud() for a profile
without a policy connector, as for Guest. Such a profile isn't a user's,
and the policies that may restrict these features can't be checked for
it. The other checks in the file call this first.

Context: We are introducing the Top Chrome WebUI profile
(crrev.com/c/8429343), which hosts Top Chrome WebUIs such as the WebUI
toolbar for the windows of all profiles. It isn't a user's profile and
has no policy connector, so these checks would crash for its frames
once the WebUI toolbar is created in it (crrev.com/c/8567399).

No behavior change for other profiles, which always have a policy
connector.

content: Record UMA for network service process wait on shutdown
📋 Metadata
commit
155110c6714dbfa439519b07fe079528f370d1ff
[ log ]
author
Achuith Bhandarkar <achuith@chromium.org>
Thu Oct 22 02:01:33 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Oct 22 02:01:33 2026
tree
05ae6b659419154b73858b7e1404bfdbad2b39ad
parent
167904d3ae89253c51ef003571fdcf1dd4d15317 [ diff ] 
content: Record UMA for network service process wait on shutdown

During browser shutdown, BrowserProcessIOThread::ProcessHostCleanUp()
waits synchronously on the main thread for the network service utility
process to exit so cookies and cache can flush to disk (up to 1 second
on ChromeOS and 10 seconds on other platforms). Currently, the wait
duration is only logged via DVLOG(1) and the WaitForExitWithTimeout()
return value is ignored.

Record Shutdown.NetworkService.WaitTime and
Shutdown.NetworkService.TimedOut so we can measure how long browser
teardown blocks on the network service and how often the wait hits the
timeout.

Bug: b:575822584, b:572927279

1d1aab652220f3d2690a0093a9ac63ca73dbe5b1 [ diff ] 
Remove expired vc-tray-mic-indicator feature flag

The vc-tray-mic-indicator flag has expired in M140. Since
kVcTrayMicIndicator was enabled by default, this CL inlines the enabled
code path in MicTestButtonContainer and removes the feature flag, its
helper function, flag metadata, and flag description.

Together with the vc-tray-title-header removal in crrev.com/c/8561222,
title_view.cc no longer uses ash_features.h, so its include is removed
as well.

TAG=agy
CONV=0cbfbbb4-686e-4a15-b736-fc588740c928

parent
0900c69230d6ebf724e2494e444fbd9f0f22b200 [ diff ] 
Feed: Use JniUniquePtr in RendererBridge

Replace the raw long mNativeSurfaceRenderer field in
FeedSurfaceRendererBridge with JniUniquePtr so JNI Zero tracks native
peer lifetime for ::feed::android::FeedSurfaceRendererBridge and
prevents use-after-free across JNI calls.

Return jni_zero::MakeUnique from the C++ initializer and invoke
JniUniquePtr.destroy() inside destroy(). The field is nulled out on
destruction, and the surface methods that call into native check it
alongside mRenderer, so they are still skipped after destroy().

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

[Direct Offers] Remove the card-linked offer type

GPay card-linked offers are no longer created or shown. This removes the
`GPAY_CARD_LINKED_OFFER` offer type, the `GPayCardLinkedOffer()` factory
and the eligible instrument ids from `AutofillOfferData`.

02506c30d197adb5eabd79b2fcce51be2a966175
parent
72e454bd901f964aa13adfdd69d6ca604f38d41c [ diff ] 
[Background Sync] Remove the expired BackgroundSync histograms

BackgroundSyncMetrics recorded twenty histograms about registrations,
sync events and wakeup tasks. Nineteen of them expired between
2022-07-31 and 2025-02-16, so the code that records them is dead. Only
BackgroundSync.Event.OneShotResultPattern is still live (it expires on
2027-04-04).

Remove the recording helpers for the expired histograms and their call
sites in BackgroundSyncManager, together with the code that only fed
them: the "could fire" and "is duplicate" enums, the start time of a
batch of sync events, and the main frame client lookup when an event
starts. RecordFailureAndPostError() no longer records anything and
becomes PostError(). Since the result pattern is now only recorded for
one-shot events, the remaining helper becomes RecordOneShotEventResult()
and the lookup at event completion is skipped for periodic events.

The barrier that runs the completion callback once a batch of events
has finished is kept; it now posts the callback directly instead of
going through OnAllSyncEventsCompleted(). The CHECK on a periodic
registration's min_interval goes away with CountRegisterSuccess(); the
same condition is already checked in Register().

Also remove the histograms.xml entries, the six enums that no other
histogram uses, and BACKGROUND_SYNC_STATUS_MAX, which only bounded the
removed histograms. HistogramsRecordedAtCompletion now checks the
one-shot result pattern instead of the removed completion histogram.

33483407de71859eb0051b8ce6e90133456bf482 [ diff ] 
[Webium] Use user profile for TopChromeWebUIProfile WebPreferences

TopChromeWebUIProfile, which exists only when
kTopChromeWebUISharedRenderer is enabled, is shared by the windows of
all profiles and has no user prefs of its own. Add
webui::SetUserProfile() and webui::GetUserProfile() in
chrome/browser/ui/webui/top_chrome/ to associate a TopChromeWebUIProfile
WebContents with the user profile it is used for, using a dedicated
WebContentsUserData. The client that creates the WebContents sets it, so
it doesn't rely on a browser and also works for WebContents held outside
a browser.

Use the user profile in these ChromeContentBrowserClient methods:
- OverrideWebPreferences()
- OverrideWebPreferencesAfterNavigation()
- WebPreferencesNeedUpdateForColorRelatedStateChanges()
- GetForcedColorsForWebContents()

The user profile must be set right after creating the WebContents,
before WebPreferences are first computed.

No behavior change when kTopChromeWebUISharedRenderer is disabled.

Thu Oct 22 01:19:44 2026
tree
504f4338c47c16a280394383c061930075ee2f05
parent
fa519bec9f151471c7039710fbcbe7aa5fbfdfba [ diff ] 
webnn: auto-reset idle compiler context from the renderer side

When a WebNN backend uses the Compiler process, the renderer holds a
WebNNCompilerContext remote for building graphs. Keeping it bound while
the page does not build graphs anymore holds the Compiler process open
unnecessarily.

This CL uses reset_on_idle_timeout() to idle reset the compiler context.
Once no graph builders are connected (and no compile calls are in
flight), the compiler context remote resets when the timeout elapsed,
letting the Compiler process shut down when none remain in use.
CreateWebNNGraphBuilder() can still reconnect on demand.

This CL also exposes reset_on_idle_timeout() on HeapMojoRemote.

4008fe484c6ad8c76fa16a15924109c747535309 [ diff ] 
[contextual-tasks] Update image context menu non-blocking privacy flow.

When the feature flag is enabled and the user uses the image context
menu entry point, the selected region only upload mode will be used, and
therefore the privacy notice does not need to be shown. The user will be
taken directly to the image search results in the side panel without a
blocking or non-blocking privacy notice. This applies whether contextual
tasks is enabled or disabled.

Previously, when contextual tasks was enabled, the feature flag had no
effect and users saw the blocking privacy notice, so this enables the
non-blocking image context menu flow for contextual tasks.

Previously, when contextual tasks was disabled, the non-blocking flow
deferred the page content upload until the user submitted a subsequent
query. This functionality is now removed so that the page content will
never be uploaded during the non-blocking flow.

Demo: http://shortn/_MdqgNknZgJ

is flaky on chrome/ci/mac64 (Mac-15): 24 failed and 117 flaky verdicts
out of 953 over the last 7 days (LUCI Analysis). Failures time out in
WaitForShow() waiting for
GlicSelectionWidgetDelegate::kAskGeminiButtonElementId.

Example failure: https://ci.chromium.org/p/chrome/builders/ci/mac64/313533

Sheriff-o-Matic blamed crrev.com/1723592 ("[PA] Enable Clang AllocToken"),
but the test passed in that build (313531) and was already failing
before it, in build 313503 at crrev.com/1723498.

tree
a265f814776429ce57891203bd404a284fc75e22
parent
6302e4036c376f4772154f175122c47147705f89 [ diff ] 
[TabStrip] Load links dropped onto a tab into that tab (2/6)

Load a link dropped onto the middle of a tab into that tab.
-Previously, a link dropped onto a tab always opened in a new tab
 before or after it.
-Now, StripLayoutHelper#getLinkDropTabToLoadInto returns the tab when
 the drop is over the middle half of it, and handleLinkDrop selects
 that tab and loads the link into it. The tab is selected first so
 that a frozen tab is restored before loading the URL.
-Drops over the leading and trailing quarters of a tab still insert a
 new tab before or after it.
-Loading into a pinned tab is allowed.

e9d9b5344524d4d09547386ef232d293b5f6589c
parent
3fc0be606dab16d17f622b127caaf3950c8db390 [ diff ] 
Add some hardening around PAC-related C++ strings passed to v8.

This adds CHECKs if the strings are destroyed while v8 holds a "lock"
on them, protecting against bugs in v8 where it destroys strings while
still using them. There's some similar hardening around blink strings.

This doesn't fix any actual bug, it just results in better behavior
when v8 bugs are encountered.

remoting: Add detailed metrics to video encoder benchmark

Extends webrtc_video_encoder_benchmark to record per-frame encode
metrics (encode latency, encoded byte size, quantizer, and keyframe
status) rather than only total encode duration.

Reports aggregate and keyframe vs. delta-frame breakdowns for:
- Encode latency percentiles (mean, p50, p90, p99, max)
- Achieved stream bitrate vs. target bitrate
- Encoded frame sizes (total, average, max)
- Quantizer / QP statistics (average, min, max)

81302a606658fcc49b4594102593d4df5f2fdc3b [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Migrate SuggestionsOmtInputAnchor JNI to JniUniquePtr

Replace raw long native pointer and manual UnregisterInputReceiver()
in SuggestionsOmtInputAnchor and SuggestionsOmtOverlayView with
JniTypeToken and JniUniquePtr<NativeOmtInputReceiverState>. Move NDK
AInputReceiver and AInputReceiverCallbacks release into
OmtInputReceiverState::~OmtInputReceiverState() so RAII handles both
early-return cleanup during registration and teardown on destroy().

[🌷] Disable test_ariaTextboxEditabilityChanges

Disable the failing accessibility event test
WebContentsAccessibilityEventsTest#test_ariaTextboxEditabilityChanges.
-Since https://crrev.com/c/8614411, the test fails consistently on
 Android release builders without DCHECKs: the event order matches the
 expectation from before that CL, not the updated one.
-The updated expectation only holds on DCHECK-enabled builds (debug
 builders and CQ trybots), so CQ can't catch the mismatch.
-Reverting the reland would re-break the other event tests it fixed, so
 only this test is disabled until its expectation works on both build
 types.

parent
cc94046c8d9076cda3ce90657665e8d00047def3 [ diff ] 
[chromecast] Remove unused AvSettings and OutputRestrictions interfaces

AvSettings was the OEM interface for HDMI/CEC/volume control, and its
only remaining Chromium user was chromecast/media/base/media_caps.cc,
which read a few EOTF and Dolby Vision bitmask constants. media_caps.cc
has not been part of any build target since crrev.com/c/5236124
("[Chromecast] Remove screen info from MediaCaps."), and nothing
includes media_caps.h. output_restrictions.h was only included by
avsettings.h. The Android java_cpp_enum target generated from
avsettings.h has no dependents; Android Cast receivers maintain their
own copy of this interface outside Chromium.

Remove the orphaned media_caps files, both headers, the java_enums
target, and the --force-media-resolution-{height,width} switches whose
only reader was media_caps.cc.

Fix some problems with WebFrameSwapTestClient

DidPropagateDisplayNoneProperty() needs to flush the mojo pipe to the
LocalFrameHost before answering, in case a property change was queued.
Some testing helpers are added on LocalFrame and LocalFrameMojoHandler
to facilitate this.

In addition, TestLocalFrameHostForFrameOwnerPropertiesChanges should
forward to the WebFrameSwapTestClient for the same frame and not its
parent, because we're already calling DidChangeFrameOwnerProperties on
the frame that the HTMLFrameOwnerElement belongs to.

Also change TestWebFrameClient::Frame() to return WebLocalFrameImpl*,
and use WeakPersistent instead of raw_ptr for its frame_ member.

parent
ce40d01e87a8e51dd7de02297d0fdcf42a7dc67f [ diff ] 
[AF] Fix flaky FieldInNonAllowlistedIframe_Aborts browser test

When <input autocomplete="cc-number"> is placed inside a cross-origin
iframe (c.com), the child frame can finish parsing and extract forms
before the parent frame (a.com) finishes initial layout and sends
SetFrameOwnerProperties(is_display_none = false) to the child renderer.
When that happens, the cc-number field is extracted with
is_visible() == false, causing
OmniboxAutofillDelegate::OnFieldTypesDetermined to log
kNoVisibleCreditCardNumberFields instead of kNonAllowlistedIframe.

Fix this by keeping cc-number in the main frame (a.com) so it is
guaranteed to be visible, placing cc-name in the allowlisted iframe
(b.com) and cc-exp in the non-allowlisted iframe (c.com), and starting
the non-allowlisted iframe navigation from the allowlisted iframe's
onload handler so the form is only complete once both iframes have
loaded.

tree
77992a0305c4faf2935b378683120804ad25e225
parent
6bb97efcc31c3df2f0db2265fcf7c06b954fdbae [ diff ] 
[BottomSheet] Support E2E bottom layout for resizable bottom sheets

Ensure bottom edge-to-edge layout works when a bottom sheet uses
HeightMode.RESIZE_CONTENT and coversBottomControls() is true.

Previously, passing getEdgeToEdgeBottomInset() to
calculateSheetTranslationY() when expanded shifted the sheet upward by
e2eBottomInset while calculateContentContainerHeight() sized the
container without e2eBottomInset, leaving a gap at the bottom of
resizable sheets.

When coversBottomControls() is true, pass 0 for the layout bottom inset
when expanded (retaining getEdgeToEdgeBottomInset() in PEEK mode so the
peek view remains lifted above the navigation bar). This allows
resizable bottom sheets to extend all the way to the bottom of the
screen and removes the need to subtract getEdgeToEdgeBottomInset() from
mContentDesiredHeight or special-case coversBottomControls() in
BottomSheetMediator.

TAG=agy
CONV=71c2ac87-85ad-4d0c-b9c5-bd178c46715d

Add no file system backends for a BrowserContext without a path
📋 Metadata
commit
6bb97efcc31c3df2f0db2265fcf7c06b954fdbae
[ log ]
author
Anna Sato <annasato@chromium.org>
Thu Oct 22 00:33:54 2026

Thu Oct 22 00:33:54 2026
tree
70c2fd4d5c2a873936f4c9a1575d4c8771f23929
parent
3e721e6d6b92e4cad27749236c09853ba08fd1fe [ diff ] 
Add no file system backends for a BrowserContext without a path

ChromeContentBrowserClient::GetAdditionalFileSystemBackends() adds
Chrome's file system backends when a StoragePartition is created, e.g.
the ChromeOS ones for external mounts and MTP devices, and the one for
Sync FileSystem. Some of them need the path of the BrowserContext to put
files under it, e.g. DeviceMediaAsyncFileUtil CHECKs that it isn't
empty.

A BrowserContext without a path keeps all its storage in memory, and has
nowhere for these backends to put their files. Add none for it.

Context: We are introducing TopChromeWebUIProfile (crrev.com/c/8429343),
which has no path. It needs a StoragePartition for the WebContents of
the Top Chrome WebUIs, which don't use these file systems. On ChromeOS,
creating it currently hits the CHECK in DeviceMediaAsyncFileUtil.

No behavior change for BrowserContexts with a path.

[html-in-canvas] Fix nested canvas drawable in filter

This patch updates `UpdateElementCanvasTransform()` to walk past
non-drawable effects when falling back to `canvas_contents.Effect()` for
a nested `content=drawable` canvas inside a drawable subtree.

When a nested `content=drawable` canvas was inside a filtered drawable
ancestor (or was itself a filtered drawable), its
`canvas_contents.Effect()` was the filter effect node
(`IsInDrawableCanvasSubtree() && !HasCanvasDrawableState()`). Top-level
drawables in the nested canvas fell back to `canvas_contents.Effect()`
because `UpdateForChildren()` on the nested canvas reset
`current_effect` to `Root()`. Parenting inner drawables to the filter
effect prevented `PaintArtifactCompositor` from decompositing the filter
into the enclosing drawable's effect and triggered a `CHECK` failure.
Walking `effect` up while `IsInDrawableCanvasSubtree() &&
!HasCanvasDrawableState()` ensures inner drawables are parented to the
enclosing drawable's effect node.

Fixed: 562108899, 575053148

a876bfec3efdc9a3a6b9a515ff26dff1b9260ba5 [ diff ] 
[html-in-canvas] Fix nested drawable under filter

This patch sets `CanvasDrawableState::content_effect` to the drawable's
parent effect [1] (`current_effect`) instead of
`canvas_fragment.ContentsEffect()`. This fixes a mistake where we
missed some updates when adding support for drawable descendants.
UpdateElementCanvasTransform (should be refactored to split out the
effect update) runs prior to UpdateEffect and updates the
`context_.current_effect` to the nearest drawable's effect. This effect
can be used directly for the canvas drawable state, rather than using
the canvas's effect. This change fixes an issue later in the pipeline
when the wrong effect was used for the drawable's recording.

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/core/paint/object_paint_properties.h;drc=3798f301b5516b1757d199eb5b64f10238ef21b1;l=151

Replace some StrCat()+String::Number() usages with Format()

Replaces several usages of StrCat() combined with String::Number() with
the more idiomatic Format() utility.

Using Format() is preferred in these cases as it:
1. Improves code readability.
2. Typically produces smaller and faster binary code.

This CL has no user-visible behavior changes.

tree
71a54bd6af41752fc035693c375b61f50fae42bc
parent
c289722e3c49af6c027da46aef80a82abc7559b9 [ diff ] 
[PA][base] Gate Lock ownership tracking on EXPENSIVE_DCHECKS_ARE_ON

On dcheck_always_on = true builds, base::Lock and
partition_alloc::internal::Lock track owning_thread_ref_ via
PlatformThread::CurrentRef() (which reads TLS viaCurrentId()) and force
out-of-line Lock::Acquire()/Release() calls on every lock acquisition
and release. Across browser_tests, lock ownership tracking and
per-thread held-lock tracking account for ~3.5-4.5% of total CPU
(~200-250 ms CPU per test).

Gate owning_thread_ref_ and GetTrackedLocksHeldByCurrentThread() in
base::Lock, base::ConditionVariable, base::SequenceCheckerImpl, and
partition_alloc::internal::Lock on EXPENSIVE_DCHECKS_ARE_ON() /
PA_BUILDFLAG(EXPENSIVE_DCHECKS_ARE_ON) instead of DCHECK_IS_ON(),
allowing Lock::Acquire() and Lock::Release() to inline directly to
LockImpl when expensive DCHECKs are disabled while preserving Clang's
compile-time -Wthread-safety annotations.

Expected cost savings: ~3.5-4.5% of total browser_tests CPU (~200-250 ms
CPU per test).

[remoting] Fix LSan leak and re-enable FileChooserChromeOsTest

FileChooserChromeOs destroys its SequenceBound<Core> asynchronously on
the UI task runner. SingleThreadTaskEnvironment was declared last in
FileChooserChromeOsTest and destroyed before file_chooser_, dropping the
Core destruction task and leaking Core under LeakSanitizer.

Declare task_environment_ first, reset file_chooser_ and flush the UI
task runner in TearDown(), and remove LeakedDanglingUntriaged from
FileChooserChromeOs::Core::ash_.

Verified with 100 local iterations (--gtest_repeat=100) and repeated
runs on linux_chromium_chromeos_asan_rel_ng (LSan) and
linux-chromeos-rel.

[Android] Disable MultiWindowAppMenuTest#testAddToGroup_showsGroupsFromOtherWindow on tablets

Since the RobustWindowManagementExperimental flag was removed, the
second window opens in split-screen, and SoftKeyboardCondition fails to
detect the soft keyboard there because it only checks the IME insets of
the host activity's window. This makes the test fail consistently on
android-15-tablet-x64-rel.

Disable the test on tablets until SoftKeyboardCondition is fixed.

Reland "[oobe] Advance directly to enrollment in Oobe.loginForTesting"

This relands crrev.com/c/8529166 (reverted in crrev.com/c/8609903).

Advancing directly to enterprise-enrollment skipped both
SkipToLoginForTesting() and AutoEnrollmentCheckScreen, leaving
kAutoEnrollmentCheckExited false under kOobeAutoEnrollmentCheckForced
and crashing in SwitchWebUItoMojo() -> AdvanceToScreen("unknown").

Set prefs::kAutoEnrollmentCheckExited in
OobeTestAPIHandler::AdvanceToScreen() when advancing directly to
EnrollmentScreenView::kScreenId, avoiding the GaiaScreenHandler async
cleanup race on SigninPartitionManager in Oobe.loginForTesting().

parent
dd27c2cac4b9d859d961c2bb805f940d9c379551 [ diff ] 
[Reading mode PDFs] Defer display node computation until debounce ends

While the PDF draw debouncer is running, OnAXTreeDistilled() now skips
computing display nodes, post-processing the selection, and drawing.
That work was discarded by the next page's distillation, making
multi-page PDF loads O(P^2). OnPdfDebounceFinished() now does it once.

tree
14f60fda3f903be5f7da1d270a4f5b20a9292e8a
parent
8e646979b93c955786e951497dbf43864134d373 [ diff ] 
[webvtt] Make video::cue read-only

https://crrev.com/c/8610568 added `-webkit-user-modify: read-only
!important` to the `video::-webkit-media-text-track-*` pseudo-elements
in `mediaControls.css`, but left `video::cue` unpinned. Because
`-webkit-user-modify` is inherited, a `STYLE` block inside a `.vtt`
file can still set `-webkit-user-modify: read-write` on the cue
background box (`VTTCueBackgroundBox`) and make it editable and
focusable.

This CL adds -webkit-user-modify: read-only !important to the video::cue
rule in mediaControls.css so that WebVTT-embedded STYLE blocks cannot
make VTTCueBackgroundBox editable or focusable.

Wed Oct 21 23:52:23 2026

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 21 23:52:23 2026
tree
c1e10eafcbdfda669d1adee81a7ded84c713b34a
parent
f1a8d2ac53cc269a55c505ce99e010b126fe994e [ diff ] 
[Indigo] Ignore initial 0x0 FrameSizeChanged before replacement renders

`IndigoImageReplacementManagerBrowserTest` tests that register a second
primary replacement (`HandlesDelayedResponseAfterNewPrimaryIsRegistered`,
`CachesGeneratedImageOnReinvocation`, and `CacheExpiresAfterLifetime`)
flakily fail with:

  Uncaught (in promise) Error: No image replacement found

followed by `mock_replacement2.WaitForRenderReplacement()` timing out.

When a replacement `<iframe>` is created and navigated cross-process to
the Indigo component extension (`chrome-extension://.../index.html`),
its `LocalFrameView` in the extension renderer is initialized with a
`0x0` size until the parent frame's layout geometry is synchronized to
the child frame via `SynchronizeVisualProperties`. When the extension
renderer process is already warm (as on the second primary replacement),
it can run its first `BeginMainFrame` before receiving the parent
frame's visual properties, emitting `FrameSizeChanged(0x0)`.

`IndigoPageActionController::FrameSizeChanged` treated this initial
`0x0` notification as the primary image becoming empty
(`kEmptyPrimaryImageSize`) and immediately called
`Reset(ResetType::kResetReplacementsAndContentScript)`, destroying the
replacement before the component extension finished initializing and
called `readyToRender()`.

Fix this by tracking whether `IndigoImageReplacement::OnReadyToRender()`
has been called and ignoring `0x0` (`frame_size.IsZero()`) frame size
notifications before the replacement has been rendered.

Ignore GPU timer timestamps that are out of sync with the CPU clock
📋 Metadata
commit
4de4318031661aa12e2d68a4c4d203907155a9f3
[ log ]
author

tree
223093f211a07d7b1ba93c37a77cf263273ed920
parent
10c670f60d0468c812203d2411f3b5ad26171257 [ diff ] 
Ignore GPU timer timestamps that are out of sync with the CPU clock

GPUTiming converts GPU timestamp query results to CPU time using an
offset computed from glGetInteger64v(GL_TIMESTAMP). This assumes both
are in the same clock domain, but some drivers violate that. For
example, nouveau returns CPU time plus a delta computed once at screen
creation for GL_TIMESTAMP, while timestamp queries use the GPU's PTIMER.
The two drift apart over the lifetime of the GPU process, so eventually
every presentation timestamp is either before the frame was drawn or in
the future. viz's SanitizePresentationFeedback() then turns every
presentation into a failure.

Among other things, this prevents the fullscreen exit bubble from ever
being presented "successfully", so its presentation watchdog exits
fullscreen 1.5s after pressing F11 once the browser has been running for
a while.

The GPU can't execute a timestamp query before it's issued or after the
result is read back, so if the converted timestamp falls outside that
range, fall back to the current time, as is already done for
GLFence-based presentation feedback.

Reproduced with ANGLE-GL on Mesa by simulating the nouveau clock drift
in GPUTiming: F11 fullscreen reverted after ~1.5s once the clocks had
drifted apart, and stays fullscreen with this change.

NOTRY=true

5c2cea4df0744b0a6f3361d3e536488d99ee2648 [ diff ] 
[base] Inline uncontended DCHECK checks in LockImpl::Try and Unlock

On POSIX/Fuchsia builds with DCHECK_IS_ON(), LockImpl::Try() and
LockImpl::Unlock() unconditionally call out-of-line BASE_EXPORT
functions dcheck_trylock_result(rv) and dcheck_unlock_result(rv) in
lock_impl_posix.cc on every lock acquisition and release. Because
Try() and Unlock() are defined in lock_impl.h and inlined into
lock.cc, condition_variable_posix.cc, and (when lock tracking is
disabled) callers of base::Lock across translation units, non-LTO
builds cannot inline these checks across translation units. In
LockImpl::Try(), the out-of-line call also forces the caller to
preserve rv in a callee-saved register across the call before
testing rv == 0.

Mark dcheck_trylock_result() and dcheck_unlock_result() ALWAYS_INLINE
in lock_impl.h so the common-case fast-path checks (rv == 0 ||
rv == EBUSY for trylock, rv == 0 for unlock) are evaluated inline,
and only call out-of-line cold helpers LogTryLockFailure(rv) and
LogUnlockFailure(rv) when an unexpected pthread error occurs. This
avoids duplicating the error conditions while keeping <ostream> out
of lock_impl.h.

In pprof CPU profiles of linux-chromeos-rel browser_tests (21-test
batch, 143.36s total CPU with dcheck_always_on = true), base::Lock
operations account for 8.68s (6.05% of total CPU), of which the
out-of-line dcheck_trylock_result() and dcheck_unlock_result() calls
account for ~0.15-0.25% of total browser_tests CPU (~8-15 ms CPU per
test).

Fix synchronous window.alwaysOnTopPopup state after window.open()
📋 Metadata

5fd3bdd82a466b943829a93269e38080c1a4b360
[ log ]
author

parent
cc443504b748027881aedec4177239c89788ca31 [ diff ] 
Fix synchronous window.alwaysOnTopPopup state after window.open()

In `blink::CreateWindow()`, `old_page->GetChromeClient().CreateWindow()`
synchronously receives `CreateNewWindowReply` from the browser and
applies `reply->visual_properties.always_on_top_popup` (which is `false`
when the site lacks the `window-management` permission). However,
`CreateWindow()` then calls `page->SetWindowFeatures(features)`, which
overwrote `always_on_top_popup_` with the unverified renderer-requested
`features.always_on_top`.

Remove `always_on_top_popup_ = features.always_on_top;` from
`Page::SetWindowFeatures()` so that `always_on_top_popup_` is only
driven by `VisualProperties` from the browser.

review whenever tests or dependencies change.

Move the messaging and private API browser tests into a local
browser_tests target in the contextual tasks extension directory and add
matching test data owners coverage.

[ContextualTasks] Log service worker messages

Contextual Tasks search communication under the rearchitecture flows
over an extension service worker port instead of webview or iframe
postMessage channels, leaving those exchanges invisible in
chrome://omnibox debug logs.

Record inbound and outbound proto messages and dropped frames in the
service worker handler under OnServiceWorkerMessage and
PostServiceWorkerMessage tags so developers can inspect full payloads
and distinguish service worker traffic from legacy webview logs.

tree
53e997b104f18770788faf839a9410e214359884
parent
a1366cdd3f43751ab7d9f817587209328794ac27 [ diff ] 
Make appearance:auto select popup background Canvas

When the <select> element has a transparent or semi-transparent
background-color, the <select> element inside the popup inherits that
transparent background-color and composites over the popup's <body>
element, which had background-color: white from picker_common.css. In
color-scheme: dark, this caused light option text to render over a white
or white-blended background with little or no contrast. Setting
body { background-color: Canvas } in the popup document makes
transparent and semi-transparent backgrounds blend with Canvas instead.

Fixed: 468205016, 565453079

da70c6c4e5ea1826d6ca548d85221a8948dd069c [ diff ] 
[glic] Scale only the cursor image in the click animation

The click animation scaled the whole #magicCursor box. The cursor chip
sits inside that box, so it shrank on every click.

Per UX, the chip should stay still. The cursor press still gives click
feedback. The chip text stays steady while people read it.

- Move the click animation, transform-origin and the animationend
  listener to #cursorImage.

- Remove the position translate from cursor-click.

- Drop --cursor-x and --cursor-y, which only the old keyframes read.

- Add a test that pauses the press at its deepest point. The cursor
  image must shrink and the chip rect must not change.

The cursor looks the same when no chip shows.
